# HKG Tmax Milestones

**Last updated:** 2026-06-21T22:00:45.911102Z  
**Primary research target:** HKG official daily Tmax, T-24 style cutoff at T-1 15:00 HKT  
**Confirmation lock:** 2024-01-01 onward remains sealed for tuning  
**Production status:** disabled

## Current Champion

The current best leakage-audited deployable research screen is `0081_rss_gate_stability_stress`, which stress-confirms the `0080` RSS-2022+ source/era specialist gate. Earlier experiments `0070` through `0073` found useful diagnostic structure and tiny causal lifts, `0074` first converted that direction into a deployable gain, `0075` refined the online residual-memory settings, `0078` added a small prior-only specialist improvement, `0079` found a stronger raw two-family specialist combo, `0080` hardened that combo, and `0081` confirmed adjacent-year stability across 2022 and 2023 without using 2024+ confirmation rows.

| Scope | Rows | Date range | MAE C | RMSE C | Status |
|---|---:|---|---:|---:|---|
| 0081 RSS-2022+ adjacent-year stress-confirmed gate | 2670 | 2000-01-02 to 2023-12-31 | 0.9438 | 1.2009 | Current best deployable research screen |
| 0080 RSS-2022+ source/era hardened specialist gate | 2670 | 2000-01-02 to 2023-12-31 | 0.9438 | 1.2009 | Same prediction rule, before adjacent-year stress confirmation |
| 0078 prior-only weak morning-warming specialist | 2670 | 2000-01-02 to 2023-12-31 | 0.9449 | 1.2018 | Previous deployable research screen |
| 0079 guarded two-family specialist combination | 2670 | 2000-01-02 to 2023-12-31 | 0.9439 | 1.2003 | Best raw MAE screen, but not hardened: slight fold/source worsening |
| 0075 refined online residual-memory corrector | 2670 | 2000-01-02 to 2023-12-31 | 0.9456 | 1.2018 | Previous deployable research screen |
| 0074 online residual-memory half-life corrector | 2670 | 2000-01-02 to 2023-12-31 | 0.9466 | 1.2028 | Previous deployable research screen |
| 0069 source/era-aware causal prior selector | 2670 | 2000-01-02 to 2023-12-31 | 0.9493 | 1.2084 | Previous deployable research screen |
| 0073 best causal source/era shrinkage | 2670 | 2000-01-02 to 2023-12-31 | 0.9490 | 1.2080 | Directionally positive but below materiality gate |
| 0073 diagnostic source/era fixed atlas | 2670 | 2000-01-02 to 2023-12-31 | 0.9451 | 1.2025 | Diagnostic only, not deployable |
| EXP-0050 headline OOF | 1460 | 2020-01-01 to 2023-12-31 | 1.2366 | 1.5714 | Separate long-history-only benchmark |

This is not close to the target competitive level yet and remains research-only. The most important current fact is that official-forecast anchoring plus causal online residual memory plus tightly guarded prior-only specialists is now the strongest deployable path on the current non-contiguous archive: press detail is usable for 2000-2004 and RSS is usable for 2021-2023 inside the sealed pre-2024 research frame. `0079` shows that ISD and Waglan sea-temperature specialists can improve raw MAE together, `0080` shows that the useful part is concentrated in the RSS 2022+ source/era slice, and `0081` confirms adjacent-year stability: `131` predictions change, full MAE improves by `0.0010958122134711035 C` versus `0078`, source/fold max deltas are `0.0`, and the changed-row deltas are negative in both 2022 and 2023. While the official forecast backfill continues, the next high-value step is to rerun the official-forecast export and re-score `0078` through `0081` once the backfill stabilizes.

### Official Forecast Archive

| Signal | Rows | Date range | MAE C | RMSE C | Interpretation |
|---|---:|---|---:|---:|---|
| RSS raw latest pre-cutoff forecast | 992 | 2021-04-14 to 2023-12-31 | 1.0158 | 1.2761 | Stronger than weather-only champion on overlapping dates |
| RSS past-only 365d bias correction | 992 | 2021-04-14 to 2023-12-31 | 1.0047 | 1.2623 | Best official-only correction in this run |
| RSS 75% official / 25% champion, 7d bias corrected | 992 | 2021-04-14 to 2023-12-31 | 0.9869 | 1.2366 | Best overlap blend so far; promising but not promotion-ready |
| Parsed press archive selected forecast, past-only 90d bias | 1678 | 2000-01-02 to 2004-08-05 | refreshed under 0012 | refreshed under 0012 | Confirms official forecast skill, but parsed archive is still partial |

The offline press export now writes `67,819` scoreable forecast-day rows under `data/datasets/05_hko_historical_rss_forecasts`, with scoreable target dates from `2000-01-01` to `2004-08-05`. The export quality gate blocks implausible target dates; the previous 1990 parser artifact is no longer scoreable.

Critical limitation: the local press archive has `324,225` `candidates` indexed from `2000-01-01` to `2026-06-20`, but raw detail HTML is currently present only for 2000-2004. Years 2005-2026 have indexed candidates but zero local raw detail HTML. It is not yet a continuous 2000-2026 forecast archive. Completing and validating the remaining raw-detail backfill remains the highest-value data task.

### Feature And Station Signal Atlas

The first scan evaluated `529` numeric leakage-eligible feature columns against target Tmax, champion signed residual, and champion absolute error. It found:

| Family | Feature count | Best target corr | Best residual corr | Best abs-error corr |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| ISD regional surface | 42 | 0.9316 | 0.1227 | 0.1585 |
| Upper air | 236 | 0.8965 | 0.1102 | 0.1467 |
| Station network | 55 | 0.9230 | 0.0986 | 0.1321 |
| Target memory | 127 | 0.8672 | 0.0921 | 0.2014 |
| Daily climate | 60 | 0.8214 | 0.0600 | 0.1571 |

Interpretation: the extra station/upper-air/weather features have real residual signal, but the raw linear correlations are modest. The path toward 0.45 likely requires official-forecast anchoring plus regime-specific residual correction, not just adding more weather-only regressors.

## Accepted Milestone Findings

- G0 environment and archival smoke test: accepted as `EXP-0001`.
- EXP-0050 long-history benchmark repair: current research champion, but not production eligible.

## Research Findings Not Yet Accepted As Milestones

- Official HKO forecast archive is now the strongest identified anchor signal on available RSS overlap.
- A simple fixed blend of official forecast and long-history champion reached `0.9869` MAE on 2021-04-14 to 2023-12-31, but that sample is shorter than the requested four full OOF years.
- Residual correction candidates exist in upper-air/surface coupling, dewpoint spread, station graph modes, and historical volatility, but none are proven as accepted model improvements yet.

This supports a next-generation regime/blend system, not a claim that weather-only features can close the gap alone.

```

New folder: `research/data_analysis/0021_failure_modes`.

| Failure thresholds | 2670 official rows | Top-20% abs-error threshold `1.60 C`; top-10% abs-error threshold `2.10 C`; worst underprediction threshold `-1.80 C`; worst overprediction threshold `1.40 C` | Diagnostic only, pre-2024 |
| Single-feature contrasts | 2204 rows | `target_roll120_mean_lag7_c` is the strongest top-10 abs-error contrast, standardized diff `-0.5296` | Big errors cluster in cooler/transition memory states |
| Feature buckets | 2720 rows | `target_roll120_mean_lag7_c` low bucket has MAE `1.2852` | Cool-memory/transition states are a clear failure-mode marker |
| Two-feature segments | 2163 rows | `daily_north_point_sea_temperature_pm_lag7_roll7` x `target_lag120_tmax_c` has MAE `1.4425` | Coastal sea temperature plus cool-memory is a sharp weak segment |
| Archetypes | 19 labels | `spring_transition+station_spread_extreme` has MAE `1.5792` | Strong candidate for richer specialist |

Interpretation: this screen finally identifies sharper official-forecast failure regimes rather than just weak global correlations. The key weak zones are spring transition, high humidity, station-spread extremes, upper-air inversion/RH combinations, and colder target-memory states. The next model work should not add more generic buckets; it should create fold-local specialists targeted specifically at these failure modes and then blend them against the official anchor.

The result reinforces that the current bottleneck is not another simple threshold rule; it is the missing continuous 2005-2020 official forecast detail plus richer local models/blends trained on a continuous archive.

```

New folder: `research/data_analysis/0026_pressure_gradient_experts`.

| Area | Rows / candidates | Strongest current finding | Status |

| Pressure blend screen | `4` candidates | `pressure_blend_inverse_mae_same_source`: MAE `0.9988`, RMSE `1.2740`, delta vs official `-0.0170` | Improves official, but does not beat `0018` |
| Feature family ranking | 6 feature sets | Pressure plane plus pressure spreads plus wind/advection is better than pressure spreads alone | Pressure-gradient value is interaction/regime-dependent |
| Leakage tests | 4 focused tests | Spread creation, ridge clipping, current-day exclusion, and same-source isolation all pass | Guarded |

Interpretation: pressure-gradient structure is real and deployable-style corrections improve the official forecast anchor, but the simple rolling ridge form still falls short of the existing `0018` prior-performance expert blend at MAE `0.9974`. The finding narrows the next path: pressure gradients should not be used as one global linear correction only. They need regime gating, nonlinear thresholding, station-network thermal interaction terms, and continuous forecast history before promotion.

| Feature family ranking | 5 feature sets | Pressure 1-day change plus calendar/monsoon and upper-air MSE/theta-e is best; full context second; wind/advection third | Confirms useful ingredients |
| Leakage tests | 3 focused tests | Smooth correction clipping, current-day exclusion, and same-source isolation all pass | Guarded |

Interpretation: replacing sparse hard interaction cells with smooth local residual experts helps, but it still does not beat the simpler `0026` pressure ridge blend or the `0018` official expert blend. This is strong negative evidence against spending more time on pressure-only local residual methods in the current partial archive. The pressure signal should be retained as a component, but the next lift likely needs either continuous 2005-2020 official forecast coverage or a broader official residual system using forecast text/range/source dynamics plus non-pressure station/upper-air/cloud features.

## Current Blockers And Gaps

1. Full 2000-2026 official forecast archive is indexed. Raw detail HTML and parsed scoreable rows now cover press target dates through 2011-09-13 after the refreshed `0044`; candidate years 2012-2026 still have zero local raw detail HTML and remain the dominant acquisition blocker.
2. RSS exact-vintage forecast rows alone do not provide four full sealed OOF years before 2024.
3. 2024-2026 confirmation rows must remain locked until a final confirmation command.
4. Residual-signal features now have a first guarded specialist conversion in `0086`, but they still need interaction tests, stack ablations, and final leakage audit before promotion.
5. The best expanded-frame official-anchor rule is now `0086` at `1.0540` MAE, improving `0.0058 C` versus the `0084` base and `0.0251 C` versus raw official forecasts on the 5,265-row expanded frame. This is real progress but still far from the `0.45 C` target.
6. Forecast-anchor analysis is currently non-contiguous: stable exported scored rows now cover 2000-01-02 through 2011-09-13 and 2021-04-14 through 2023-12-31, leaving a 3,500-day gap from 2011-09-14 through 2021-04-13.
7. Simple past-only official-bias correction improves only modestly; stronger regime/expert structure is required.
8. Past-only expert blending is the best correction screen so far but still only improves official same-row MAE by `0.0184 C`.
9. The multistation/attribute matrix found real residual signals, but the best simple past-only bucket candidate improves official same-row MAE by only `0.0066 C`; richer regime-specific experts are required.
10. The refreshed regime-expert factory finds station pressure/dew-spread value, but the best regime blend improves official same-row MAE by only `0.0112 C` and does not beat the `0018` expert blend.
11. Failure-mode segmentation has now been converted into fold-local specialists, but the best specialist blend improves official same-row MAE by only `0.0084 C`, weaker than the `0018` prior-performance blend.
12. The current evidence says threshold-style specialists alone are not enough; the next lift likely requires continuous forecast coverage and richer local residual models per regime.
13. Composite stacking of official, ladder, analog, regime, and failure experts improves official MAE but does not beat the existing `0018` champion; adding weak specialists without stronger gating dilutes the best stack.
14. The long-history calendar-adjusted atlas says pressure-gradient and spatial station structure are major information channels; the current expert stack does not yet exploit them with a dedicated local residual model.
15. A first deployable-style pressure-gradient ridge screen improved official MAE by `0.0170 C`, but still did not beat the `0018` champion; pressure gradients need regime-specific/nonlinear treatment rather than another global linear correction.
16. Pressure-regime pair cells show strong diagnostic residual separation, but hard prior-only pair buckets improve official MAE by only `0.0121 C`; the next step needs smoother gated/local models rather than sparse cell matching.
17. Smooth pressure-gated local experts improve official MAE by only `0.0136 C`; this is weaker than the earlier pressure ridge blend and confirms pressure-only residual modeling is not enough on the current non-contiguous archive.
18. Official residual source/text/range dynamics are now structured and tested, but the best deployable bucket improves official MAE by only `0.0120 C`; single-channel metadata/text bucket corrections are not enough without continuous forecast history and richer multi-signal residual modelling.
19. Multi-signal local nearest-neighbour residual modelling improves official MAE by `0.0143 C`, but the full feature set underperforms narrower official text/weather matching; broad Euclidean similarity is diluting signal and should be replaced with regime-gated specialist selection.
20. Deterministic regime-gated specialist selection ties the best `0030` standalone specialist but does not improve it; small hand-built regimes are too sparse/imbalanced for reliable prior-performance selection on the current non-contiguous archive.
21. Residual archetype discovery creates a new best MAE of `0.9962`, but the improvement over `0018` is still only `0.0012 C`; the next lift requires smoother archetype-specific corrections and broader continuous forecast coverage, not another hard two-condition bucket screen.
22. Smooth residual archetype specialists improve the best MAE to `0.9935`; forecast jump-up/revision-momentum is now the strongest correction family, but the current gain is still incremental and needs cluster-centroid soft gating plus stronger expert stacking.
23. Cluster-centroid soft gating improves the best blended MAE to `0.9934` and RMSE to `1.2663`; centroid geometry is complementary in blends, but the best single centroid specialist is weaker than the targeted `0033` forecast-jump specialist.
24. Forecast revision momentum deep dive confirms that range-widening/jump-up revision subfamilies are strong but not enough alone: best 0035 single MAE is `0.9935`, best 0035 blend MAE is `0.9946`, and the 0034 centroid blend remains the current best on the stable 2,670-row scored frame.
25. Revision-centroid stack ablation improves the best MAE to `0.9927`; the improvement is small but establishes that 0033 smooth forecast-jump specialists and 0034 centroid specialists have complementary MAE signal under strict date-prior selection.
26. Stack trust meta-features improve the best MAE to `0.9898`; the best deployable selector is a same-source trust rule keyed by `forecast_max_vs_prior7_mean_source_c`, proving that forecast-history state can decide which expert family to trust.
27. Forecast-history state deepening tested 51 meta features and 252 trust candidates but did not improve beyond the simpler 0037 forecast-vs-prior rule; richer jump/staleness/range/text states are explanatory, but too sparse or redundant on the current non-contiguous scored forecast archive.
